\displaystyle\left.\frac{{\rm d}}{{\rm d}x}{\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-\mu}(-x)\right|_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle x\to-1^{+}$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle x\to-1^{+}$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle x\to-1^{+}$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle x\to-1^{+}$}}}=0,\qquad\Re\mu>2,

\displaystyle\left.\frac{{\rm d}}{{\rm d}x}\left[\frac{1}{\big(1-x^{2}\big)^{\frac{\mu}{2}}}{\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-\mu}(-x)\right]\right|_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle x\to-1^{+}$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle x\to-1^{+}$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle x\to-1^{+}$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle x\to-1^{+}$}}}=-\frac{\nu(\nu+1)}{2^{\mu+1}\Gamma(\mu)}.

(2.47)

We next define an odd Ferrers function
{\mathsf{f}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{\mu}\colon(-1,1)\to{\mathbf{C}}
, by the difference

\displaystyle{\mathsf{f}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{\mu}(x):={\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{\mu}(-x)-{\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{\mu}(x).

(2.48)

Remark 2.12
.

This difference is directly proportional to the difference of Ferrers functions of the second kind through
[
22
, p. 170]

\displaystyle{\sf Q}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{\mu}(-x)-{\sf Q}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{\mu}(x)=\frac{\pi}{2}\cot\big(\tfrac{\pi}{2}(\nu+\mu)\big)\big({\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{\mu}(-x)-{\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{\mu}(x)\big)=\frac{\pi}{2}\cot\big(\tfrac{\pi}{2}(\nu+\mu)\big){\mathsf{f}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{\mu}(x).

(2.49)

We note that (
2.48
) vanishes if
{\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{\mu}(x)
is even. This occurs if
\nu+\mu
is a non-negative even integer, or if
\nu-\mu
is a negative odd integer (see
[
26
, Chapter 5, p. 187]
). Similarly, the Ferrers function of the second kind difference (
2.49
) vanishes if
\nu+\mu
is a positive odd integer, or if
\nu-\mu
is a negative odd integer. For our purposes (
2.48
) will suffice, since in our applications this function will not vanish identically.

Lemma 2.13
.

Let
\nu\in{\mathbf{C}}
,
\Re\big(\alpha\pm\frac{\mu}{2}\big)>0
. Then

\displaystyle\int_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle-1$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}}^{1}\big(1-x^{2}\big)^{\alpha-1}{\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-\mu}(\pm x)\mathrm{d}x=\frac{\pi\Gamma\big(\alpha+\frac{\mu}{2}\big)\Gamma\big(\alpha-\frac{\mu}{2}\big)}{2^{\mu}\Gamma\big(\alpha+\frac{\nu+1}{2}\big)\Gamma\big(\alpha-\frac{\nu}{2}\big)\Gamma\big(\frac{\nu+\mu+2}{2}\big)\Gamma\big(\frac{\mu-\nu+1}{2}\big)}.

(2.50)

Proof.

Let
I
denote the integral in (
2.50
), with positive sign in the argument of the Ferrers function. Insert the hypergeometric representation
of the Ferrers function of the first kind (
2.35
), and make the substitution
\xi=(1-x)/2
. This converts
I
to a Mellin transform, namely after re-mapping
\xi\mapsto x
,

\displaystyle I=\frac{2^{2\alpha-1}}{\Gamma(1+\mu)}\int_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle 0$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle 0$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{1}x^{\alpha+\frac{\mu}{2}-1}(1-x)^{\alpha-\frac{\mu}{2}-1}\,\mbox{}_{2}F_{1}\!\left(\genfrac{}{}{0.0pt}{}{-\nu,\nu+1}{1+\mu};x\right)\mathrm{d}x.

Using
[
12
, equation (7.512.5)]
, we can evaluate the integral in terms of a
{}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle 3$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle 3$}}}F_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}(1)
, namely

\displaystyle I=\frac{2^{2\alpha-1}\Gamma\big(\alpha+\frac{\mu}{2}\big)\Gamma\big(\alpha-\frac{\mu}{2}\big)}{\Gamma(1+\mu)\Gamma(2\alpha)}\,\mbox{}_{3}F_{2}\!\left(\genfrac{}{}{0.0pt}{}{-\nu,\nu+1,\alpha+\frac{\mu}{2}}{1+\mu,2\alpha};1\right).

The
{}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle 3$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle 3$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle 3$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle 3$}}}F_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle 2$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}(1)
in question can be evaluated using Whipple’s sum
[
27
, equation (16.4.7)]
for
\Re\big(\alpha\pm\frac{\mu}{2}\big)>0
. The invariance of
I
with argument of the Ferrers function of the first
kind as
-x
follows by direct substitution. Hence the proof of the lemma is demonstrated.
∎

Corollary 2.14
.

Let
\Re{\mu}>-1
. Then

\displaystyle\int_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle-1$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle-1$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}}^{1}\big(1-x^{2}\big)^{\frac{\mu}{2}}{\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-\mu}(\pm x)\mathrm{d}x=\frac{2^{\mu+1}\Gamma(\mu+1)}{\Gamma(\mu-\nu+1)\Gamma(\nu+\mu+2)}.

(2.51)

Proof.

Using Lemma
2.13
and properties of gamma function completes the proof.
∎

2.4.2
Large degree asymptotics of Ferrers functions

Theorem 2.15
.

Let
\mu\geq 0
bounded,
\delta\in(0,\pi)
,
\theta\in\left(0,\pi-\delta\right]
, and the envelope functions for
J_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}
,
Y_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}
being the real-valued ones given by
[
27
, equations (2.8.32)–(2.8.34)]
. Then uniformly we have, as
\nu\rightarrow\infty
,

\displaystyle\mathsf{P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-\mu}(\cos\theta)=\frac{1}{\nu^{\mu}}\sqrt{\frac{\theta}{\sin\theta}}\left\{J_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}\big(\big(\nu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)\theta\big)+{\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{{1}}{{\nu}}\right)}\operatorname{env}J_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}\big(\big(\nu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)\theta\big)\right\},

(2.52)

\displaystyle\mathsf{P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{\mu}(\cos\theta)=\nu^{\mu}\sqrt{\frac{\theta}{\sin\theta}}\left[\cos(\pi\mu)\left\{J_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}\big(\big(\nu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)\theta\big)+{\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{{1}}{{\nu}}\right)}\operatorname{env}J_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}\big(\big(\nu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)\theta\big)\right\}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\hphantom{\mathsf{P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{\mu}(\cos\theta)=}{}-\sin(\pi\mu)\left\{Y_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}\big(\big(\nu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)\theta\big)+{\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{{1}}{{\nu}}\right)}\operatorname{env}Y_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}\big(\big(\nu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)\theta\big)\right\}\right],

(2.53)

\displaystyle\mathsf{Q}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-\mu}(\cos\theta)=-\frac{\pi}{2\nu^{\mu}}\sqrt{\frac{\theta}{\sin\theta}}\left\{Y_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}\big(\big(\nu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)\theta\big)+{\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{{1}}{{\nu}}\right)}\operatorname{env}Y_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}\big(\big(\nu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)\theta\big)\right\},

(2.54)

\displaystyle\mathsf{Q}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{\mu}(\cos\theta)=-\frac{\pi\nu^{\mu}}{2}\sqrt{\frac{\theta}{\sin\theta}}\left[\sin\left(\pi\mu\right)\left\{J_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}\big(\big(\nu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)\theta\big)+{\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{{1}}{{\nu}}\right)}\operatorname{env}J_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}\big(\big(\nu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)\theta\big)\right\}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\hphantom{\mathsf{Q}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{\mu}(\cos\theta)=}{}+\cos(\pi\mu)\left\{Y_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}\big(\big(\nu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)\theta\big)+{\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{{1}}{{\nu}}\right)}\operatorname{env}Y_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}\big(\big(\nu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)\theta\big)\right\}\right],

(2.55)

\displaystyle{\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-\mu}(-\cos\theta)=\frac{1}{\nu^{\mu}}\sqrt{\frac{\theta}{\sin\theta}}\left[\cos(\pi(\nu-\mu))\left\{J_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}\big(\big(\nu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)\theta\big)+{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{1}{\nu}\right)\operatorname{env}J_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}\big(\big(\nu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)\theta\big)\right\}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\hphantom{{\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-\mu}(-\cos\theta)=}{}+\sin(\pi(\nu-\mu))\left\{Y_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}\big(\big(\nu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)\theta\big)+{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{1}{\nu}\right)\operatorname{env}Y_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}\big(\big(\nu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)\theta\big)\right\}\right],

(2.56)

\displaystyle{\sf Q}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-\mu}(-\cos\theta)=-\frac{\pi}{2\nu^{\mu}}\sqrt{\frac{\theta}{\sin\theta}}\left[\sin(\pi(\nu-\mu))\left\{J_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}\big(\big(\nu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)\theta\big)+{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{1}{\nu}\right)\operatorname{env}J_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}\big(\big(\nu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)\theta\big)\right\}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\hphantom{{\sf Q}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-\mu}(-\cos\theta)=}{}-\cos(\pi(\nu-\mu))\left\{Y_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}\big(\big(\nu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)\theta\big)+{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{1}{\nu}\right)\operatorname{env}Y_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}\big(\big(\nu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)\theta\big)\right\}\right].

(2.57)

Proof.

Using
[
27
, equations (14.15.11) and (14.15.12)]
, the uniform asymptotic approximations follow from simplifying the ratio of gamma functions in (
2.40
), (
2.41
), via (
2.2
), and setting
x=\cos\theta
. The asymptotic approximations (
2.56
), (
2.57
) follow using (
2.41
), (
2.42
), (
2.52
), (
2.54
).
∎

Corollary 2.16
.

Let
\mu\geq 0
be bounded. Then for the odd Ferrers function (
2.48
), we have as
0<\nu\rightarrow\infty
,

\displaystyle{\mathsf{f}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-\mu}(\cos\theta)=\frac{1}{\nu^{\mu}}\sqrt{\frac{\theta}{\sin\theta}}\left[(\cos(\pi(\nu-\mu))-1)\left\{J_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}\big(\big(\nu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)\theta\big)+{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{1}{\nu}\right)\operatorname{env}J_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}\big(\big(\nu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)\theta\big)\right\}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\hphantom{{\mathsf{f}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-\mu}(\cos\theta)=}{}+\sin(\pi(\nu-\mu))\left\{Y_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}\big(\big(\nu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)\theta\big)+{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{1}{\nu}\right)\operatorname{env}Y_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}\big(\big(\nu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)\theta\big)\right\}\right],

(2.58)

uniformly for
\theta\in\big(0,\tfrac{1}{2}\pi\big]
, and

\displaystyle{\mathsf{f}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-\mu}(\cos\theta)=\frac{1}{\nu^{\mu}}\sqrt{\frac{\pi-\theta}{\sin\theta}}\bigg[(1-\cos(\pi(\nu-\mu)))

\displaystyle\hphantom{{\mathsf{f}}_{\nu}^{-\mu}(\cos\theta)=}{}\times\left\{J_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}\big(\big(\nu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)(\pi-\theta)\big)+{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{1}{\nu}\right)\operatorname{env}J_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}\big(\big(\nu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)(\pi-\theta)\big)\right\}

(2.59)

\displaystyle\left.\hphantom{{\mathsf{f}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-\mu}(\cos\theta)=}{}-\sin(\pi(\nu-\mu))\left\{Y_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}\big(\big(\nu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)(\pi-\theta)\big)+{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{1}{\nu}\right)\operatorname{env}Y_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}\big(\big(\nu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)(\pi-\theta)\big)\right\}\right],

uniformly for
\theta\in\big[\frac{1}{2}\pi,\pi\big)
.

Proof.

For (
2.58
), combine (
2.52
), (
2.56
). For (
2.59
) replace
\theta
by
\pi-\theta
in (
2.58
) and observe that
{\mathsf{f}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-\mu}(\cos(\pi-\theta))={\mathsf{f}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-\mu}(-\cos\theta)=-{\mathsf{f}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-\mu}(\cos\theta)
.
∎

Similar approximations for the Ferrers conical functions read as follows.

Theorem 2.17
.

Let
\mu\geq 0
bounded,
\delta\in(0,\pi)
,
\theta\in(0,\pi-\delta]
. Then uniformly, we have, as
0<\tau\rightarrow\infty
,

\displaystyle\mathsf{P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}}^{-\mu}(\cos\theta)=\frac{1}{\tau^{\mu}}\sqrt{\frac{\theta}{\sin\theta}}I_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}(\tau\theta)\left\{1+{\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{{1}}{{\tau}}\right)}\right\},

(2.60)

\displaystyle\mathsf{P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}}^{\mu}(\cos\theta)=\tau^{\mu}\sqrt{\frac{\theta}{\sin\theta}}\left[I_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}(\tau\theta)\left\{1+{\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{{1}}{{\tau}}\right)}\right\}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\hphantom{\mathsf{P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}}^{\mu}(\cos\theta)=}{}+\frac{2}{\pi}\sin(\pi\mu)K_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}(\tau\theta)\left\{1+{\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{{1}}{{\tau}}\right)}\right\}\right],

(2.61)

\displaystyle\mathsf{Q}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}}^{-\mu}(\cos\theta)=\frac{1}{\tau^{\mu}}\sqrt{\frac{\theta}{\sin\theta}}\left[{\rm e}^{\mp{\rm i}\pi\mu}K_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}(\tau\theta)\left\{1+{\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{{1}}{{\tau}}\right)}\right\}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\hphantom{\mathsf{Q}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}}^{-\mu}(\cos\theta)=}{}\mp{{\frac{{\rm i}\pi}{2}}}I_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}(\tau\theta)\left\{1+{\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{{1}}{{\tau}}\right)}\right\}\right],

(2.62)

\displaystyle\mathsf{Q}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}}^{\mu}(\cos\theta)=\tau^{\mu}\sqrt{\frac{\theta}{\sin\theta}}\left[\cos(\pi\mu)K_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}(\tau\theta)\mp{{\frac{{\rm i}\pi}{2}}}I_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}(\tau\theta)\right]\left\{1+{\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{{1}}{{\tau}}\right)}\right\},

(2.63)

\displaystyle\mathsf{P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}}^{-\mu}(-\cos\theta)=\frac{{\rm e}^{\pi\tau}}{\pi\tau^{\mu}}\sqrt{\frac{\theta}{\sin\theta}}K_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}(\tau\theta)\left\{1+{\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{{1}}{{\tau}}\right)}\right\},

(2.64)

\displaystyle\mathsf{Q}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-{\frac{1}{2}}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}}^{-\mu}(-\cos\theta)=\mp\frac{{\rm i}{\rm e}^{\pi\tau}}{2\tau^{\mu}}\sqrt{\frac{\theta}{\sin\theta}}K_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}(\tau\theta)\left\{1+{\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{{1}}{{\tau}}\right)}\right\}.

(2.65)

Proof.

Under the hypotheses of the theorem, the approximation (
2.60
), along with

\displaystyle\hat{\mathsf{Q}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}}^{-\mu}({\cos\theta})=\frac{1}{\tau^{\mu}}\sqrt{{\frac{\theta}{\sin\theta}}}K_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}({\tau\theta})\left(1+{\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{{1}}{{\tau}}\right)}\right),

(2.66)

where
\hat{\mathsf{Q}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}}^{-\mu}(x)
is defined by
[
27
, equation (14.20.2)]
, follow directly from
[
27
, equations (14.20.17) and (14.20.18)]
. Now from
[
27
, equation (14.20.3)]
we have the connection formula

\displaystyle\hat{\mathsf{Q}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}}^{-\mu}(x)=A({\mu,\tau})\mathsf{P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}}^{-\mu}(x)+B(\mu,\tau)\mathsf{P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}}^{-\mu}({-x}),

(2.67)

where
A,B\colon[0,\infty)^{2}\to{\mathbf{R}}
, are defined by

\displaystyle A(\mu,\tau):=\frac{\pi{\rm e}^{-\pi\tau}\sin({\mu\pi})\sinh({\tau\pi})}{2\big({\cosh^{2}({\tau\pi})-\sin^{2}({\mu\pi})}\big)},\qquad B(\mu,\tau):=\frac{\pi\big({{\rm e}^{-\pi\tau}\cos^{2}({\mu\pi})+\sinh({\tau\pi})}\big)}{2\big({\cosh^{2}({\tau\pi})-\sin^{2}({\mu\pi})}\big)}.

Hence from (
2.39
), (
2.67
) we obtain

\displaystyle\mathsf{Q}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}}^{-\mu}(x)=G({\mu,\tau})\mathsf{P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}}^{-\mu}(x)+H(\mu,\tau)\hat{\mathsf{Q}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}}^{-\mu}(x),

(2.68)

where

\displaystyle G:=-\frac{A+BC}{BD},\qquad H:=\frac{1}{BD},\qquad C:=\sin(\pi({\rm i}\tau-\mu)),\qquad D:=\frac{2}{\pi}\cos(\pi({\rm i}\tau-\mu)).

Then from solving (
2.68
) for
\hat{\mathsf{Q}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}}^{-\mu}(x)
, and simplifying and approximating
G(\mu,\tau)
,
H(\mu,\tau)
for large
{\tau}
, one finds after some calculation that

\displaystyle\mathsf{Q}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}}^{-\mu}(x)={\rm e}^{-{\rm i}\pi\mu}\hat{\mathsf{Q}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}}^{-\mu}(x)\big(1+\mathcal{O}\big(\tau^{-1}\big)\big)-{{\frac{{\rm i}\pi}{2}}\mathsf{P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-\frac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau$}}}^{-\mu}(x)}\big(1+\mathcal{O}\big(\tau^{-1}\big)\big).

(2.69)

We then insert (
2.60
), (
2.66
) into (
2.69
) and with
x=\cos\theta
, arrive at (
2.62
). Finally, using the connection formulas (
2.40
), (
2.41
)
(with
\nu=-\tfrac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau
), along with (
2.1
), (
2.60
), (
2.62
), we establish (
2.61
), (
2.63
). For (
2.60
), (
2.61
), note (
2.38
). Also note that in
each of (
2.61
), (
2.62
), the two terms of
\big(1+\mathcal{O}\big(\tau^{-1}\big)\big)
do not factor as they do in (
2.60
), (
2.61
) because there may be cancellations in the two terms which would then leave the result untrue. The asymptotic approximations (
2.64
), (
2.65
) follow using (
2.42
), (
2.43
), (
2.60
), (
2.62
).
∎

Corollary 2.18
.

Let
\mu\geq 0
be bounded. Then for the odd Ferrers conical function
(
(
2.48
) with
\nu=-\tfrac{1}{2}+{\rm i}\tau)
, as
0<\tau\rightarrow\infty
,

\displaystyle{\mathsf{f}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{2}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle-\tfrac{1}{2}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle-\tfrac{1}{2}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-\tfrac{1}{2}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}}^{-\mu}(\cos\theta)=\frac{1}{\tau^{\mu}}\sqrt{\frac{\theta}{\sin\theta}}\left[\frac{{\rm e}^{\pi\tau}}{\pi}K_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}(\tau\theta)-I_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}(\tau\theta)\right]\left\{1+{\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{{1}}{{\tau}}\right)}\right\},

(2.70)

uniformly for
\theta\in\big(0,\frac{1}{2}\pi\big]
, and

\displaystyle{\mathsf{f}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{2}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle-\tfrac{1}{2}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle-\tfrac{1}{2}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-\tfrac{1}{2}\pm{\rm i}\tau$}}}^{-\mu}(\cos\theta)=-\frac{1}{\tau^{\mu}}\sqrt{\frac{\pi-\theta}{\sin\theta}}\left[\frac{{\rm e}^{\pi\tau}}{\pi}K_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}(\tau(\pi-\theta))-I_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}(\tau(\pi-\theta))\right]\left\{1+{\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{{1}}{{\tau}}\right)}\right\},

(2.71)

uniformly for
\theta\in\big[\frac{1}{2}\pi,\pi\big)
.

Proof.

For (
2.70
) combine (
2.60
), (
2.64
). The proof of (
2.71
) is similar to that of (
2.59
).
∎

2.4.3
Ferrers function addition theorems

In
[
7
, Theorem 4.1]
, an addition theorem is derived for a fundamental solution of the Laplace–Beltrami operator on a positive constant sectional curvature space, the
d
-dimensional hypersphere. That addition theorem was for the Ferrers function the second kind
{\sf Q}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}^{-\mu}(\cos\Theta)/\sin^{\mu}\Theta
, where

\displaystyle\cos\Theta=\cos\theta\cos\theta^{\prime}+\sin\theta\sin\theta^{\prime}\cos\gamma.

(2.72)

In this subsection we present generalizations of that addition theorem for
{\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{\pm\mu}(\pm\cos\Theta)/\sin^{\mu}\Theta
,
{\sf Q}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{\pm\mu}(\pm\cos\Theta)/\sin^{\mu}\Theta
.

Theorem 2.19
.

Let
\mu\in{\mathbf{C}}
such that
{\Re}\,\mu>-{\tfrac{1}{2}}
,
\mu\neq 0
,
\nu\in{\mathbf{C}}\setminus-{\mathbf{N}}
,
\gamma\in{\mathbf{R}}
,
\theta,\theta^{\prime}\in(0,\pi)
such that

\displaystyle\tan\big({{\tfrac{1}{2}}\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle<$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}}\big)\tan\big({{\tfrac{1}{2}}\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle>$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}}\big)<1.

(2.73)

Then provided the Ferrers functions of the second kind are defined,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sin^{\mu}\Theta}{\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{\mu}(\cos\Theta)=\frac{2^{\mu}\Gamma(\mu)}{(\sin\theta\sin\theta^{\prime})^{\mu}}

\displaystyle\hphantom{\frac{1}{\sin^{\mu}\Theta}{\sf P}_\nu^\mu(\cos\Theta)=}{}\times\sum_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n=0$}}}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}(n+\mu){\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-(\mu+n)}(\cos\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle<$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}){\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{\mu+n}(\cos\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle>$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}})C_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}^{\mu}(\cos\gamma),

(2.74)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sin^{\mu}\Theta}{\sf Q}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{\mu}(\cos\Theta)=\frac{2^{\mu}\Gamma(\mu)}{(\sin\theta\sin\theta^{\prime})^{\mu}}

\displaystyle\hphantom{\frac{1}{\sin^{\mu}\Theta}{\sf Q}_\nu^\mu(\cos\Theta)=}{}\times\sum_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n=0$}}}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}(n+\mu){\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-(\mu+n)}(\cos\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle<$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}){\sf Q}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{\mu+n}(\cos\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle>$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}})C_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}^{\mu}(\cos\gamma),

(2.75)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sin^{\mu}\Theta}{\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-\mu}(\cos\Theta)=\frac{2^{\mu}\Gamma(\mu)}{(\sin\theta\sin\theta^{\prime})^{\mu}}\sum_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n=0$}}}^{\infty}\Big[(-1)^{n}(n+\mu)(\nu+\mu+1)_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\mu-\nu)_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}

\displaystyle\hphantom{\frac{1}{\sin^{\mu}\Theta}{\sf P}_\nu^{-\mu}(\cos\Theta)=}{}\times{\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-(\mu+n)}(\cos\theta){\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-(\mu+n)}(\cos\theta^{\prime})C_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}^{\mu}(\cos\gamma)\Big],

(2.76)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sin^{\mu}\Theta}{\sf Q}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-\mu}(\cos\Theta)=\frac{2^{\mu}\Gamma(\mu)}{(\sin\theta\sin\theta^{\prime})^{\mu}}\sum_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n=0$}}}^{\infty}\Big[(-1)^{n}(n+\mu)(\nu+\mu+1)_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\mu-\nu)_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}

\displaystyle\hphantom{\frac{1}{\sin^{\mu}\Theta}{\sf Q}_\nu^{-\mu}(\cos\Theta)=}{}\times{\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-(\mu+n)}(\cos\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle<$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}){\sf Q}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-(\mu+n)}(\cos\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle>$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}})C_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}^{\mu}(\cos\gamma)\Big],

(2.77)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sin^{\mu}\Theta}{\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-\mu}(-\cos\Theta)=\frac{2^{\mu}\Gamma(\mu)}{(\sin\theta\sin\theta^{\prime})^{\mu}}\sum_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n=0$}}}^{\infty}\Big[(n+\mu)(\nu+\mu+1)_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\mu-\nu)_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}

\displaystyle\hphantom{\frac{1}{\sin^{\mu}\Theta}{\sf P}_\nu^{-\mu}(- \cos\Theta)=}{}\times{\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-(\mu+n)}(\cos\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle<$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}){\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-(\mu+n)}(-\cos\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle>$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}})C_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}^{\mu}(\cos\gamma)\Big],

(2.78)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sin^{\mu}\Theta}{\sf Q}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-\mu}(-\cos\Theta)=\frac{2^{\mu}\Gamma(\mu)}{(\sin\theta\sin\theta^{\prime})^{\mu}}\sum_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n=0$}}}^{\infty}\Big[(n+\mu)(\nu+\mu+1)_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\mu-\nu)_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}

\displaystyle\hphantom{\frac{1}{\sin^{\mu}\Theta}{\sf Q}_\nu^{-\mu}(-\cos\Theta)=}{}\times{\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-(\mu+n)}(\cos\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle<$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}){\sf Q}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-(\mu+n)}(-\cos\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle>$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}})C_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}^{\mu}(\cos\gamma)\Big],

(2.79)

where in all but (
2.76
)
\theta\neq\theta^{\prime}
. For
\mu=0
, see Corollary
2.23
.

Proof.

See Appendix
A
.
∎

Remark 2.20
.

If one is interested in the above addition theorems which correspond to the Ferrers functions with
\nu=\mu
, then one can directly use (
2.74
), (
2.75
).
If one examines the behavior of (
2.76
), when
\nu=\mu
, it is seen that only the
n=0
term in the sum survives. However, one can see through
[
27
, equation (14.4.18)]
, that the left-hand side exactly matches the
n=0
term on the right-hand side. Note that the left-hand side of (
2.77
) is defined for
\nu=\mu
, but (except for
n=0
) the terms in the series on the right-hand side are undefined in this case. However, the limit of the series exists as
\nu\to\mu
, and is given by Corollary
2.21
below. Similar limiting results can be obtained if
\nu-\mu\in{\mathbf{N}}
but we do not pursue this.

The addition theorems for the Ferrers function of the second kind with
\nu=-\mu
can be obtained in two different ways. These Ferrers functions appear in the study of a fundamental solution of the Laplace–Beltrami operator on the
d
-dimensional
R
-radius hypersphere. See (
4.11
) below and
[
3
,
5
,
7
]
.

Corollary 2.21
.

Let
\mu\in{\mathbf{C}}
such that
{\Re}\,\mu>-{\tfrac{1}{2}}
,
\mu\neq 0
,
\theta,\theta^{\prime}\in(0,\pi)
,
\gamma\in{\mathbf{R}}
, (
2.72
) and (
2.73
) hold with
\theta\neq\theta^{\prime}
. Then

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sin^{\mu}\Theta}{\sf Q}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}^{-\mu}(\cos\Theta)=\frac{\pi\tan(\pi\mu)}{2^{\mu+1}\Gamma(\mu+1)}+\frac{\sqrt{\pi}\,\sec(\pi\mu)}{\mu 2^{\mu}\Gamma\big(\mu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)(\sin\theta\sin\theta^{\prime})^{\mu}}

\displaystyle\hphantom{\frac{1}{\sin^{\mu}\Theta}{\sf Q}_\mu^{-\mu}(\cos\Theta)=}{}\times\sum_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n=0$}}}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}(n+\mu){\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}^{-n-\mu}(\cos\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle<$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}){\sf Q}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}^{n+\mu}(\cos\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle>$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}})C_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}^{\mu}(\cos\gamma)

(2.80)

\displaystyle\hphantom{\frac{1}{\sin^{\mu}\Theta}{\sf Q}_\mu^{-\mu}(\cos\Theta)}{}=\frac{-\pi\cot(\pi\mu)}{2^{\mu+1}\Gamma(\mu+1)}+\frac{\pi^{\frac{3}{2}}\csc(\pi\mu)}{\mu 2^{\mu+1}\Gamma\big(\mu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)(\sin\theta\sin\theta^{\prime})^{\mu}}

\displaystyle\hphantom{\frac{1}{\sin^{\mu}\Theta}{\sf Q}_\mu^{-\mu}(\cos\Theta)=}{}\times\sum_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n=0$}}}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}(n+\mu){\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}^{-n-\mu}(\cos\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle<$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}){\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mu$}}}^{n+\mu}(\cos\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle>$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}})C_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}^{\mu}(\cos\gamma),

(2.81)

where the first equality is valid except when
\mu
is a half odd integer, and the second equality is valid except when
\mu
is an integer.

Proof.

First start with (
2.75
), let
\nu=\mu
, and apply the connection relation (
2.41
) to the left-hand side of the equation only. The result (
2.80
) immediately follows using
[
27
, equation (14.5.18)]
. The derived formula is clearly undefined for
\mu
being a half odd integer, but is otherwise valid. Next start with (
2.74
), let
\nu=\mu
, and apply the connection relation (
2.40
), to the left-hand side of the formula only and
[
27
, equation (14.5.18)]
. The derived formula (
2.81
) is clearly undefined for
\mu
being an integer, but is otherwise valid.
∎

In order to obtain the addition theorems for the Ferrers function of the second kind when the order is equal to the negative degree, and is either an integer or a half odd integer, one can then use the above corollary. The following formulae, specializations of Corollary
2.21
, are exactly those results which are derived in
[
7
, Theorems 3.1 and 4.1]
for a fundamental solution of the Laplace–Beltrami operator on the
d
-dimensional
R
-radius hypersphere.

Corollary 2.22
.

Let
k\in{\mathbf{N}}
,
m\in{\mathbf{N}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle 0$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle 0$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}
,
\theta,\theta^{\prime}\in(0,\pi)
,
\gamma\in{\mathbf{R}}
, (
2.72
) and (
2.73
) hold with
\theta\neq\theta^{\prime}
. Then

\displaystyle\log\cot\big(\tfrac{1}{2}\Theta\big)=\log\cot\big(\tfrac{1}{2}\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle>$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}\big)

\displaystyle\hphantom{\log\cot\big(\tfrac{1}{2}\Theta\big) =}{}+\sum_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n=1$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n=1$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n=1$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n=1$}}}^{\infty}\frac{2^{n}}{n}\left[\frac{\tan\big(\tfrac{1}{2}\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle<$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}\big)}{\sin(\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle>$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}})}\right]^{n}\bigl[\cos^{2n}\big(\tfrac{1}{2}\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle>$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}\big)-(-1)^{n}\sin^{2n}\big(\tfrac{1}{2}\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle>$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}}\big)\bigr]T_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\cos\gamma),

(2.82)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sin^{k}\Theta}{\sf Q}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}^{-k}(\cos\Theta)=\frac{\sqrt{\pi}(-1)^{k}}{k2^{k}\Gamma\big(k+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)(\sin\theta\sin\theta^{\prime})^{k}}

\displaystyle\hphantom{\frac{1}{\sin^{k}\Theta}{\sf Q}_k^{-k}(\cos\Theta)=}{}\times\sum_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n=0$}}}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}(n+k){\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}^{-n-k}(\cos\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle<$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}){\sf Q}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle k$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle k$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle k$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}^{n+k}(\cos\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle>$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}})C_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}^{k}(\cos\gamma),

(2.83)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sin^{m+\frac{1}{2}}\Theta}{\sf Q}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle m+\frac{1}{2}$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle m+\frac{1}{2}$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle m+\frac{1}{2}$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle m+\frac{1}{2}$}}}^{-m-\frac{1}{2}}(\cos\Theta)=\frac{(-1)^{m}\pi^{\frac{3}{2}}}{(2m+1)2^{m+\frac{3}{2}}m!(\sin\theta\sin\theta^{\prime})^{m+\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}\times\sum_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n=0$}}}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}(2n+2m+1){\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle m+\frac{1}{2}$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle m+\frac{1}{2}$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle m+\frac{1}{2}$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle m+\frac{1}{2}$}}}^{-n-m-\frac{1}{2}}(\cos\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle<$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}){\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle m+\frac{1}{2}$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle m+\frac{1}{2}$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle m+\frac{1}{2}$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle m+\frac{1}{2}$}}}^{n+m+\frac{1}{2}}(\cos\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle>$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}})C_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}^{m+\frac{1}{2}}(\cos\gamma).

(2.84)

Proof.

First substitute
\mu=k\in{\mathbf{N}}
in (
2.80
) which produces (
2.83
). Next, substituting
\mu=m+\tfrac{1}{2}
,
m\in{\mathbf{N}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle 0$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle 0$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}
, in (
2.81
), to produce (
2.84
). Then take the limit as
\mu\to 0
in (
2.80
) using (
2.4
) which produces (
2.82
).
∎

Corollary 2.23
.

Let
\nu\in{\mathbf{C}}\setminus-{\mathbf{N}}
,

\displaystyle{\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}(\cos\Theta)=\sum_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n=0$}}}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}\epsilon_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}{\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-n}(\cos\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle<$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}){\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{n}(\cos\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle>$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}})T_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\cos\gamma),

(2.85)

\displaystyle{\sf Q}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}(\cos\Theta)=\sum_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n=0$}}}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}\epsilon_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}{\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-n}(\cos\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle<$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}){\sf Q}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{n}(\cos\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle>$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}})T_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\cos\gamma),

(2.86)

\displaystyle{\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}(-\cos\Theta)=\sum_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n=0$}}}^{\infty}\epsilon_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(-\nu)_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\nu+1)_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}{\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-n}(\cos\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle<$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}){\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-n}(-\cos\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle>$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}})T_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\cos\gamma),

(2.87)

\displaystyle{\sf Q}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}(-\cos\Theta)=\sum_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n=0$}}}^{\infty}\epsilon_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(-\nu)_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\nu+1)_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}{\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-n}(\cos\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle<$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}){\sf Q}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-n}(-\cos\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle>$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}})T_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\cos\gamma),

(2.88)

where
\epsilon_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}=2-\delta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n,0$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n,0$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n,0$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n,0$}}}
.

Proof.

Taking the limit as
\mu\to 0
in (
2.74
), (
2.75
), (
2.78
), (
2.79
), and using (
2.4
) produces the result.
∎

Note that the same result can be obtained by using (
2.76
), (
2.77
), using connection formulas in
[
27
, equations (14.9.3) and (14.9.4)]
.
Similarly we can use the addition Theorem
2.19
to expand the functions on the left-hand sides in terms of Legendre polynomials
\big(\mu=\tfrac{1}{2}\big)
. In this case, the Ferrers functions on the left-hand side reduce to trigonometric functions
[

, equation (54.5.11)–(14.5.14)]
.

Corollary 2.24
.

Let
\theta,\theta^{\prime}\in(0,\pi)
,

, (

) and (

) hold with

. Then

\displaystyle\frac{\cos\big(\big(\nu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)\Theta\big)}{\sin\Theta}=\frac{\pi}{2\sqrt{\sin\theta\sin\theta^{\prime}}}\sum_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n=0$}}}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}(2n+1){\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-n-\frac{1}{2}}(\cos\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle<$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}){\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{n+\frac{1}{2}}(\cos\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle>$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}})P_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\cos\gamma),

\displaystyle\frac{\sin\big(\big(\nu+\tfrac{1}{2}\big)\Theta\big)}{\sin\Theta}=-\frac{1}{\sqrt{\sin\theta\sin\theta^{\prime}}}\sum_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n=0$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n=0$}}}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}(2n+1){\sf P}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{-n-\frac{1}{2}}(\cos\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle<$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle<$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle<$}}}){\sf Q}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\nu$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\nu$}}}^{n+\frac{1}{2}}(\cos\theta_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle>$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle>$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle>$}}})P_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\displaystyle n$}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{$\textstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\cos\gamma).

), (

), with

and

, produces the result.
